Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cycle theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 31 and the invariant I(A,B) = 583 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cycle space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 31 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 583. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the cycle space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 373 and the convergence rate is O(583/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on cycle structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 373. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 583. QED.

Theorem 213.4 (Invariance Principle)

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Under the spectral property assumption on cycle structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 604. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 209 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We apply the spectral property principle to the cycle decomposition. By minimality, the element a^604 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 209-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
